# ki-coordination split: rule inventory

Stage 2 of the [standard-split plan](standard-split-plan.md#proving-no-rule-is-lost), proof step 1. The inventory lists every normative sentence, list item, table row and quotation in [`standards-coordination.md` at `480e774c`](https://github.com/knowledgeislands/ki-agentic-harness/blob/480e774c/skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md), including the `output-style`, `hook-line`, `hook-pointer`, `run-report`, `footer-report` and `review-independence` source blocks. The banner and heading examples are left to the hard-token coverage test.

A sentence counts as normative when it carries a modal or quantifier (must, never, only, every, always, should, cannot, do not, no, none, each, may, unless, before, after, until, required) or opens with an imperative verb. Each list item, table row and quoted line is listed whole. The extraction is mechanical, so a few descriptive sentences are included; they take the disposition **kept** like any other.

Each entry takes one disposition as the split moves it: **kept** (new file and anchor), **merged** (duplicate of entry N), **to sources** (history only) or **dropped** (reason, owner-approved). The design note is deleted when the split graduates. The mechanical split in `fb842b12` moved every entry unchanged, so each now reads **kept** with its new file and section; later stages overwrite the disposition of each entry they move or merge. Stage 4 (history out) overwrote 234 dispositions: history to `sources.md`'s decision register, past shapes to `standards-coordination-migration.md`. Stage 5 (terse rewrite) moved no entry between files and kept every anchor, so each **kept** link still resolves; the entries it changed carry a `stage 5:` note, and [Stage 5 dispositions](#stage-5-dispositions) gives each unit's commit and review findings.

| R0021 | The responsive coordinator | body | sentence | L97 | Every rule in this standard serves one principle, which Kris set for coordinators on 2026-10-13 in these words: | kept: [standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator); history to sources: RESPONSIVE-PRINCIPLE |
| R0022 | The responsive coordinator | body | quote | L99 | It's imperative that you do not do the work directly and that you delegate that work to subagents so that you remain responsive. And if I send you a message, it doesn't preempt any work that you are already doing. This is a fundamental requirement of the delegation capability. | to sources: RESPONSIVE-PRINCIPLE |
| R0023 | The responsive coordinator | body | sentence | L103 | **The coordinator never does substantive work itself.** A coordinating session dispatches, decides with the owner and reports. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator) |
| R0024 | The responsive coordinator | body | sentence | L104 | **Every unit of work goes to a detached background agent.** Through `ki agent`, in its own session, surviving anything that happens to the coordinator. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator) |
| R0025 | The responsive coordinator | body | sentence | L105 | **An owner message never pre-empts work in flight.** It steers: the coordinator folds it into running and queued work and answers within one short turn. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator) |
| R0026 | The responsive coordinator | body | sentence | L105 | A background agent stops only when the owner names it and says stop. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator) |

| R0028 | The responsive coordinator | body | sentence | L109 | Three layers support these rules: the opener carries the explicit coordinator role; the runtime hook conditionally points to this standard without detecting that role; and the counted turn budget and self-check bound each coordinator turn. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#the-responsive-coordinator) |

| R0096 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L205 | **Brief-writing:** the coordinator writes only a short brief citing the owner's decisions, into the folder of the focus area the run serves, `<focus-id>/<run>.brief.md`, or `briefs/<run>.md` in a package that declares no focus areas, under the `ki-work-package` focus areas. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0097 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L206 | **Self-check:** before each tool call, the coordinator asks whether it is a quick check or bookkeeping; if not, it delegates. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |

| R0099 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L207 | It never pastes a prompt, brief or other long text inline in a command: prompt files are written out of sight, with `ki agent new` or a one-line copy of a prepared file. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0100 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L207 | Replies carry no raw tool output; the coordinator says in plain language what the result means, under Quiet tools. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0101 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L207 | Never issue a coordinator command that may wait for a permission prompt; put deletion and housekeeping in a bounded run or the existing close operation, and report a denied action instead of waiting unattended. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0102 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L207 | The shapes that trip a runtime-specific check, Claude Code's built-in `rm` safety check under bypass mode, with no Codex counterpart observed yet, are an `rm` with a glob, a shell-variable or `$(...)` target, or a target under a workspace directory, alone or inside a compound command or `sh -c`; | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |

| R0104 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L207 | This grants no bypass of a permission gate. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0105 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L208 | It stops or aborts a background agent or piece of work only when the owner expressly says to stop or abort it, naming the work. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0106 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L208 | Because it is never mid-way through long work, it answers an owner message promptly. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0107 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L209 | "UPDATE-CADENCE / yes: add to ki-coordination - full Summary only when all dispatched work has finished or on request; otherwise timed one-to-two-line updates; a blocking query raised in a single line; the resume line shown only at rest; a report that does not repeat unchanged queries still carries the full stacked reply block for every open query". | to sources: UPDATE-CADENCE |
| R0108 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L209 | The closing thread line now replaces send and resume lines, and the reply block is gone, so: while work lies with a worker the coordinator gives timed updates of one or two lines, timestamped, when it launches work and when an agent finishes, and none while it waits, under QUIET-LISTEN below; it raises a blocking query the moment it arises, in a single line, rather than holding it for the next report; it gives the full Summary in the owner report form only when all dispatched work has finished, when the owner asks for one, or as the gap Summary below; and every banner closes with its thread line. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness); history to sources: UPDATE-CADENCE, QUIET-LISTEN; stage 5: review S5-C02 applied, the update comes when an agent finishes |
| R0109 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L209 | Take each timed update's local time from the clock in that turn, never from recalled labels. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0110 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L210 | So the first reply after 30 minutes or more without an owner message, while queries are with the owner, answers the owner's message first and then gives the Summary, with every open query in full, so the owner returning after a break sees everything waiting on them in one place. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness); history to sources: GAP-SUMMARY |
| R0111 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L211 | **Heartbeat:** the heartbeat runs only while work lies with a worker, and a run finishing is itself the wake. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0112 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L211 | "HEARTBEAT / while work runs, the thread's background check also wakes it every 5 minutes for a one-to-two-line timed update with each agent's current step; add it to the ki-coordination update cadence and the hook line, and apply it in this thread now", and replaced its timed updates under QUIET-LISTEN on 31 October 2026: | to sources: HEARTBEAT, QUIET-LISTEN |
| R0113 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L211 | "we don't need it to give an update unless it actually gets new information that's landed". | to sources: QUIET-LISTEN |
| R0114 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L211 | So while any of its items lies with a run, the coordinator keeps one background check armed, a detached loop under the `ki-delegation` monitoring rules that spends no model time and exits only when a run in this thread's group finishes, with no timed bound. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness); history to sources: HEARTBEAT, QUIET-LISTEN |
| R0115 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L211 | When it exits, the coordinator wakes, reads `ki agent status`, handles the finished agent in one timed line and, while runs remain, re-arms the check; it gives no timed update while it waits. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |

| R0118 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L211 | Kris agreed OOS-WAKE as recommended on 2026-10-20, "OOS-WAKE - agreed as recommended": the owner may start a detached `ki agent` watcher with an autonomy window, and when a run finishes inside that window the watcher resumes the coordinator by the work's id and exact label under Picking work up. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness); history to sources: OOS-WAKE |

| R0122 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | **Inbox watch:** a coordinating thread listens for its messages only when the owner asks it to. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0123 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | "stop the wake" or "stop listening" ends listening until the owner asks again, and a thread never asked does not listen. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0124 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | "for a thread which is listening ... we don't need it to give an update unless it actually gets new information that's landed". | to sources: QUIET-LISTEN |
| R0125 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | The watcher is one long-running background loop, a detached shell loop that spends no model time: every couple of minutes it fetches and fast-forwards local `main` silently and compares the Git object of the work's `messages.md` with the one it started from, reading no content, and it exits only on news, a new line in `messages.md` or a run in this thread's group finishing; a pull that cannot fast-forward also ends it, as news needing a resolve. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0126 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | It has no timed bound while idle, so a quiet period produces no wake, no tool calls and no chat line. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0127 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | On a wake the thread speaks only about what landed: on a new line the coordinator acts on it under Messages between work, commits, re-arms and tells the owner in one timed line what it did, and on a finished run it receives the run in one timed line and re-arms. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0128 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | A thread never holds more than one loop; after a compaction or a recovery it checks whether one is armed before arming another. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0129 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | A thread whose runtime has no proven way for the watcher's exit to wake it checks its inbox only at owner messages and run events, and shows itself as not listening. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0130 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L212 | A shared shell command for the watcher and each runtime's wake are still open questions for the owner. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |
| R0131 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L213 | **Listening:** a banner whose thread has the listening capability, `listening_enabled: true` in its `state.md`, shows `👂 listening` under Banner's mode lines while the watcher is armed, `listening_status: on`, and `🔇 not listening` otherwise, `listening_status: off`: while draining, from `pause`, `done for now` or `drain` until the drain ends, because a drain stops listening, On hold and paused, while work Snoozing, the worker's idle hold included, keeps showing `👂 listening`, after "stop the wake" or "stop listening", or where the runtime cannot be woken. | kept: [standards-listening.md#inbox-watch](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#inbox-watch) |

| R0134 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L214 | Stop starting unattended work at expiry, explicit return or "stop the wake", then report; an ordinary steering message does not cancel the window. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#away-grants-and-scheduled-wakes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#away-grants-and-scheduled-wakes) |

| R0136 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L214 | A question put to the owner during the window is asked in plain text in the thread, never through a blocking question prompt, waits a couple of minutes at most and, unanswered, is parked as a query for the return report with nothing done on it, under Unattended windows; silence is never consent. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#away-grants-and-scheduled-wakes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#away-grants-and-scheduled-wakes) |
| R0137 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L214 | An unattended window promises only the authorised attempt: report usage-limit stops, missed processing and failed runs on return, and never mark a trigger as processed work. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#away-grants-and-scheduled-wakes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#away-grants-and-scheduled-wakes) |

| R0140 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L216 | So agents that touch the same files are chained, each launched with its wait on the one before it under Coordination, so that no two edit a file at once; agents working in different repositories run in parallel; and about three agents run per piece of coordinating work at once, the rest waiting in the run queue in priority order. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness); history to sources: CONCURRENCY; stage 5: review S5-C01 applied, agents kept as the unit of concurrency |
| R0141 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L218 | Follow the coordinating-work standard (skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md) and the Work Process note it cites. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0142 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L218 | Hard rules: 1) delegate every unit of work to a detached background agent and stay responsive, answering within one short turn; 2) an owner message never pre-empts work in flight: stop an agent only when the owner names it and says stop; 3) while work lies with a worker, give a timed one-to-two-line update when you launch work and when a run finishes, keeping one background check armed that wakes you only when a run finishes, with no timed bound, and no heartbeat otherwise, while the inbox watcher, the same loop, stays armed only where the owner asked the thread to listen, or it is a Rig host sync thread, and the work is not draining, On hold or Finished, unless the owner asked the hold to listen, and wakes you only on a new message line or a finished run; 4) give every Summary in its summary shape and every re-bootstrap and hold report in the owner-report shape, heading and form line to glyph banner, with no reply block, a Summary and a re-bootstrap report restating every open query in full, end any other reply that moves the mark, changes the state or answers where the work stands with the banner, and read each spoken answer back in the decisions log; every medium- or low-confidence query, and any high-confidence destructive, deletion or publication one, goes to one blind review before it reaches the owner; 5) commit your own work and the work package by explicit path after every finished change; sync after each finished change and at least every 15 minutes under the standing push rule, fast-forward only, never forced or --no-verify, stopping on divergence; act on each messages.md line, IMPORTANT first, then in queue order, then remove it. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0143 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L220 | On supported owner-message events it emits only a short conditional pointer; after compaction it points explicitly coordinating sessions to Re-anchoring after compaction. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0144 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L220 | A declaration, cwd, package, run-home binding or session id alone never selects coordination. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0145 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L220 | Unknown selection causes no package enumeration, package reads or method-history scan. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |
| R0146 | Coordinator responsiveness | body | sentence | L220 | The user-environment manager registers the hook; this change adds no runtime marker or store. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinator-responsiveness) |

| R0148 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L226 | A coordination thread may have two capabilities, as Kris set under THREAD-CAPABILITIES on 31 October 2026: | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities); history to sources: THREAD-CAPABILITIES |

| R0150 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L228 | Each thread records its capabilities in four `state.md` fields under the `ki-work-package` state file, as Kris set under FOOTER-CAPABILITY-STATUS-2, relayed on 1 November 2026: | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities); history to sources: FOOTER-CAPABILITY-STATUS-2 |
| R0151 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L228 | If `syncing_enabled` is true, `listening_enabled` must be true, because a syncing thread is always a listening thread. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities) |
| R0152 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L228 | The coordinator sets `_enabled` when the owner's word or the agreed role grants the capability and clears it only when the owner withdraws it; it sets `_status` at every arm, stop, drain, hold and resume. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities) |
| R0153 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L230 | At a wake it makes at most one small tidy, within its turn budget: it keeps `state.md`'s front matter and next steps true; prunes received reports and spent briefs under the `ki-work-package` tidy rules; strikes a decision, or reduces it to a one-line pointer, once a durable home carries it, under the decisions life cycle; keeps `discussions.md` free of answered entries; and raises what it cannot settle as a query. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities) |
| R0154 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L230 | It says nothing about a tidy unless something needs the owner. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities) |
| R0155 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L230 | It never edits another work's package, though it may point out a problem in one with a line in that work's `messages.md`. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities) |
| R0156 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L231 | It repeats each pass until the Mac and GitHub are clean and level, or until something needs judgement, and only then waits for its next slot, as Kris set under SYNC-UNTIL-CLEAN the same day: | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities); history to sources: SYNC-UNTIL-CLEAN |
| R0157 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L231 | "a resync doesn't finish until it's got to a point of being clean". | to sources: SYNC-UNTIL-CLEAN |
| R0158 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L231 | It stays quiet on quiet passes, under SYNC-QUIET: nothing when nothing changed, at most one short line when something did, and a full report only for something that needs judgement or the owner. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities); history to sources: SYNC-QUIET |
| R0159 | Thread capabilities | body | sentence | L233 | Host sync has no banner line, as Kris decided under SYNC-LINE-OFF at about 18:00 GMT on 4 November 2026: remove the sync mode line from the banner entirely, now, because Agora keeps the machines in sync through each machine's household work package, so the line no longer says anything true and useful. | kept: [standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-listening.md#thread-capabilities); history to sources: SYNC-LINE-OFF |
| R0160 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L237 | Every report it gives is an owner report, whose sections, queries and banner are never shortened by any output style, proxy shaping block or communication level. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0161 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L239 | **Questions:** queries reach the owner in the Summary by default; on request use the interactive pass, one decision per turn, its context in chat Markdown and then a short question card, with plain chat Markdown alone where the runtime has no card. | kept: [standards-queries.md#questions](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#questions) |
| R0162 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L240 | **Heading and banner:** every Summary and every re-bootstrap report opens with the report heading and its state form line, so each reads cold and several pieces of work read side by side, and closes with the glyph banner and its thread line. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0163 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L241 | **Heading levels:** in every form the report heading is the one `#` heading, in the message and in a saved file alike; the sections (`Summary since <time>`, `Status`, `Done`, `In Progress`, `Findings`, `Queries`) are `##` headings, and each finding, each query, each topic sub-heading and the banner heading is `###`. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0164 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L241 | No other heading levels appear. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0165 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L242 | **Self-contained:** no Summary or other report says "unchanged, see earlier", "as before" or otherwise points back to an earlier report. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0166 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L242 | What is still running and what is still open is restated each time, because the owner reads each one cold and the earlier message may be gone. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0167 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L242 | Every Summary, including the gap Summary, and every re-bootstrap report restates every open query in full. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0168 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L243 | That Summary is the last message the owner sees until they return. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0169 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L245 | **State file:** every send, receive, mark and hold rewrites the state file's front matter, `state.md`, under Picking work up, so that the work package alone says how the work is picked up. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0170 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L245 | The work file, `work.md`, changes only when the work's definition does. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0171 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L246 | **Links:** every roadmap record a report names is linked by its full identifier to its local file, under Linking records, and a pruned record is linked as `[<FULL-ID> (pruned)](https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>/blob/<last revision>/<path>)`. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#links-and-wording](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#links-and-wording) |
| R0172 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L247 | **Wording:** plain language, and "background agents", never "helpers". | kept: [standards-reporting.md#links-and-wording](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#links-and-wording); stage 5: review S5-R01 applied, background agents for workers, runs for their groups, never helpers |
| R0173 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L247 | When the owner must paste a message into another session, the coordinator gives it in a code block ready to copy. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#links-and-wording](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#links-and-wording) |
| R0174 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L248 | **Decisions:** the coordinator records each owner decision in the work's decisions log, `+/_WORK/<id>/decisions.md`, before launching the work it authorises, in the turn the decision arrives, and verifies any later quotation from the transcript file, under the two rules that section sets. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0175 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L248 | It strikes, points or keeps each entry at the mark under the decisions life cycle. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |
| R0176 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L250 | **Claims:** before a brief or a Summary rests on a path, an ownership claim or the premise of an instruction, the coordinator, or the run it sends, checks it directly, and reads back in one line any direction it finds ambiguous before acting on it. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) |

| R0178 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L251 | **Owner queue:** Kris agreed OWNER-QUEUE on 2026-10-20 with checks, "yes, less piling up on me makes sense", read back as "the `ki-coordination` audit counts each work's records awaiting acceptance and open queries and warns over about eight, and the banner shows the count; acceptance fixes and backlog-clearing work stay allowed over the cap". | to sources: OWNER-QUEUE |
| R0179 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L251 | So each piece of Project work is capped at eight roadmap records awaiting acceptance and at eight open queries. | kept: [standards-territory.md#owner-queue](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#owner-queue); history to sources: OWNER-QUEUE |
| R0180 | Coordinating practices | body | sentence | L251 | Over either cap the coordinator launches no new build or planning run until the owner catches up; acceptance fixes and work that clears the backlog stay allowed. | kept: [standards-territory.md#owner-queue](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#owner-queue) |
| R0181 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L255 | Queries reach the owner in full in the Summary, and after a Summary that lists open queries the coordinator also takes them in an **interactive pass**, as it does on the owner's request, because, as Kris added to SUMMARY-MODEL on 4 November 2026, "I'm probably not going to be able to go back and read everything"; the pass after the Summary that closes a pass asks only queries that pass did not. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass); history to sources: SUMMARY-MODEL; stage 5: review S5-Q01 applied, either trigger alone starts the pass |
| R0182 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L255 | The pass never batches: it asks exactly one decision at a time, and each query takes two replies, so that the owner sends nothing between questions. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0183 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L272 | **Second reply.** Woken by the timer, the coordinator's reply holds only the card, through the runtime's question tool, such as `AskUserQuestion`: | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0184 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L274 | **Options:** each a short label, the recommended one first, its label carrying `(recommended, <confidence> confidence)`. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0185 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L275 | **Descriptions:** one or two sentences under each option saying what it means, the recommended option's opening with its `Why:`. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0186 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L276 | **Own words:** the card's free-text box serves for the owner's own words, so no option offers them. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0187 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L277 | **Pairs:** two related questions may share one card, each with a short header; otherwise one question per card. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0188 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L278 | **After the answer.** The coordinator logs it in the decisions log, then goes back to step 1, whose read-back opens the next query's first reply. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0189 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L279 | **Why.** Orca folds everything in a reply under "Worked for" except its last text, and it does not show text written after a card's answer within the same reply. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0190 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L279 | So the context must be the first reply's last text, the card must stand alone in its own reply, and the read-back travels with the next context rather than after the answer. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0191 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L282 | Where the runtime has no question card, or no way for a timer to wake the coordinator, the coordinator asks in plain chat Markdown alone: one reply carries the read-back, the context and **The options**, numbered, each with a bold name and what it means, the recommended one first carrying _(recommended, <confidence> confidence)_ and a one-line _Why:_, closing with "Reply with 1, 2 or 3, or your own words.", and then stops and waits. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0192 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L282 | An unattended window keeps its own rule and asks in plain text, never through a card, because a card blocks the session. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0193 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L284 | The coordinator never asks the next query's card in the same reply as an answer's read-back. | kept: [standards-queries.md#interactive-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#interactive-pass) |
| R0194 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L284 | Kris set the one-decision rule as INTERACTIVE-QUERIES on 31 October 2026, after a question tool showed a bare question with no context during Acquire intake that day, and refined it the same day as QUERY-TURNS: | to sources: INTERACTIVE-QUERIES, QUERY-TURNS |
| R0195 | Interactive pass | body | sentence | L284 | "Put each query in the chat as normal Markdown, starting with a counter line ... | to sources: QUERY-TURNS |

| R0198 | Goal-setting pass | body | sentence | L288 | "An owner-invoked pass, "goals" or `/ki-coordination goals`, walking each focus area one at a time to confirm or reword its goal and order, then writing `work.md`, logging Kris's words and closing with a Status." The coordinator walks `work.md`'s Focus list in order, one area at a time, in the interactive pass's two-reply shape: it reads back the area's line, which says its goal, and its place in the order, and asks the owner to confirm, reword, reorder, add or retire it. | kept: [standards-queries.md#goal-setting-pass](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-queries.md#goal-setting-pass); history to sources: GOAL-SETTING |

| R0205 | Banner | body | sentence | L302 | A report shows its state in rich text, under the note's Banner section, which Kris set with BANNER-GLYPH and REPLY-BLOCK on 18 October and TARGET-GLYPH the same day: the glyphs come first and the last glyph is whose move it is, a settled state shows its holder's glyph alone, an arrow appears only in a handoff state or in the line saying which hold or handoff With worker is heading for, and `→ 🎯 Handoff to <target>` serves every target, the target's name saying whether it is other work, a run or a person. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner); history to sources: BANNER-GLYPH, REPLY-BLOCK, TARGET-GLYPH |
| R0206 | Banner | body | sentence | L302 | Kris appears as 👤 everywhere, and every other handoff target, other work, a run or a person, as 🎯. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner); history to sources: GLYPH-OWNER |
| R0207 | Banner | body | sentence | L302 | The block-letter frames retired with the model; a report written before 18 October keeps its frame, a report written before GLYPH-OWNER keeps 🧑 for Kris and 👤 for a target, and anything that reads past reports accepts every one of these. | kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#banners-in-past-reports](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#banners-in-past-reports) |
| R0208 | Banner | body | sentence | L302 | Every coordinator renders the heading and the banner the same way, whether it has just bootstrapped, re-bootstrapped or run for days: the examples below are the shapes, and a banner that differs from them is wrong. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0209 | Banner | body | sentence | L304 | **When it appears.** Every Summary, re-bootstrap and hold report closes with the banner, and so does any other coordinator reply that moves the mark, changes the work's state or answers where the work stands, even when it is not a full report, as Kris set on 2026-10-19 under BANNER-ON-STATE, after a reply to "whats waiting on me? / mark" on held work gave none. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner); history to sources: BANNER-ON-STATE |
| R0210 | Banner | body | sentence | L304 | A plain answer that does none of these needs none. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0211 | Banner | body | sentence | L308 | `↪️ draining → ⏸️ On hold` from the owner's `pause` or `done for now` until the drain under Putting work on hold lands the hold, or `↪️ draining → 💤 Snoozing (on hold)` from `snooze` until the snooze lands; | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0212 | Banner | body | sentence | L310 | `↪️ finishing → 👤 Handoff to supervisor` once the coordinator has decided to send the work to the owner and is only letting the runs in flight finish; | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0213 | Banner | body | sentence | L311 | `↪️ finishing → 🎯 Handoff to <target>` likewise before a handoff to other work, a run or a person. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0214 | Banner | body | sentence | L313 | The line stands in the banner only, never in the heading or the form line, which show the state alone, `🤖 With worker`. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0215 | Banner | body | sentence | L313 | It is not a state or a move: the work is With worker until the target lands, the heading's `at` stays the time of the last move, and when the target lands the line goes and the heading shows the target state. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |
| R0216 | Banner | body | sentence | L313 | In the counts and the list nothing changes shape: the runs still finishing count and list as `[running]`, nothing is launched from the queue while the line stands, and the target is never a count or an item, so a drain towards a hold reads `🤖 2 running` with its `[running]` items until it reads `⏸️ drained`. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#banner](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#banner) |

| R0286 | Banner | body | sentence | L471 | **Identity changed:** the work's id, level, label or run name changed in this thread, as when a package moves from `hnr.prj.email-routing` to `hnr.reg.email-routing`: the session's name, run and opener no longer match `work.md`, and only a fresh opener sets all three again. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0287 | Banner | body | sentence | L472 | **Repeated compaction:** the session has been compacted twice or more: each re-anchoring rebuilds from a summary of a summary, and a fresh bootstrap from the work package is more faithful than another. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0288 | Banner | body | sentence | L473 | **Method moved on:** since the thread opened, the method changed its opener, the hook line or the output style, which the coordinator checks under Refreshing this guidance: a session keeps the style and opener it loaded at start, and only a fresh session loads the current ones natively. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0289 | Banner | body | sentence | L486 | It is the second named exception to the rule that a report carries no send, resume or paste line, beside the bootstrap `Rename thread:` line under Opening work. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0290 | Banner | body | sentence | L486 | It is absent otherwise: never for length, age, a change of state, a hold or a model switch alone. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0291 | Banner | body | sentence | L486 | A coordinator gives it in each report that closes with the banner while its trigger holds, and once the owner declines it, not again until a new trigger arises. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0292 | Banner | body | sentence | L486 | A hold report, a run report and a conversational reply never carry it; picking work up still follows the work package, and the owner closes the old session. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#banner-detail) |
| R0293 | Re-anchoring after compaction | body | sentence | L548 | A compaction keeps a coordinator's session but replaces its transcript with a summary, so the coordinator re-anchors from durable state before it replies; a conversational session re-reads its work package, if it has one, and carries on with a conversational reply. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction) |
| R0294 | Re-anchoring after compaction | body | sentence | L548 | The owner may choose to compact a long session using the runtime's compaction facility, keeping the work id, Working rules block, mark, open Needs tags, running and queued agents and messages acted on. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction) |
| R0295 | Re-anchoring after compaction | body | sentence | L548 | No send or resume prompt is emitted before or after compaction; a second compaction instead warrants the restart suggestion. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction) |
| R0296 | Re-anchoring after compaction | body | sentence | L550 | Whether the compaction came from the owner or from the runtime, the coordinator then, before answering anything else: | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction) |
| R0297 | Re-anchoring after compaction | body | sentence | L554 | as territory work, lists every item in its territory's `+/_WORK_REVIEW/` areas and carries the `REVIEW-HOLD` query under Work awaiting review while any holds anything; | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction) |
| R0298 | Re-anchoring after compaction | body | sentence | L559 | Re-anchoring moves no mark. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#re-anchoring-after-compaction) |
| R0299 | Surfaces | body | sentence | L563 | A coordinator may receive a message from other work through its work package's `messages.md`, which travels through Git, or from a run through the run's inbox, `~/.local/state/ki/agents/<run>/inbox.md`, written with `ki agent send <run> <line>`, which dates the line and names the sending run, and which the run's next `ki agent status` shows; where the runtime can message another live session on the same machine, as Claude Code can and Codex cannot, the sender may also nudge a live session with one runtime message naming the entry. | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0300 | Surfaces | body | sentence | L563 | The rule, which Kris approved on 2026-10-14 with "approve the surfaces rule and the ki agent status follow-on", is that **a backend reply is not visible progress**: a reply to a nudge, or an inbox acknowledgement, counts as progress only once it is on a surface the owner looks at. | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces); history to sources: SURFACES-RULE |
| R0301 | Surfaces | body | sentence | L563 | The receiving coordinator writes what it did into its next owner-facing turn and its state file; the sender keeps the item under its Needs tag, saying "acked by backend, not yet visible", until that happens. | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0302 | Surfaces | body | row | L565 | Surface \| Carries \| Who writes it \| When | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0303 | Surfaces | body | row | L567 | Transcript \| Owner turns and the coordinator's own replies; nothing injected \| The coordinator, in its owner report \| Every owner-facing turn names messages received and acted on since the last one | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0304 | Surfaces | body | row | L568 | Work package \| `work.md` and `state.md` front matter, which the observatory reads; the `messages.md` queue; parked tags in `discussions.md` \| The coordinator, under the work-package standard; other work writes only its own `messages.md` line \| `state.md` at every send, receive, mark and hold; `work.md` when the definition changes | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0305 | Surfaces | body | row | L569 | Work status \| `ki work status --format json`, schema `ki/work-status/v1`, and the five views \| `ki`, reading every work package, and every single-file work file for the tolerance window \| On demand; the Observatory reads only this | kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#single-file-work-package-shapes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#single-file-work-package-shapes) |
| R0306 | Surfaces | body | row | L570 | Notification \| Message received, acknowledgement sent, session recovered, session errored \| Not this standard \| The notification record in Knowledge Islands Principal owns the channel | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0307 | Surfaces | body | sentence | L572 | A sender that only nudged without writing the `messages.md` line or inbox entry has not sent the message. | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0308 | Surfaces | body | sentence | L572 | The inbox is machine-local; a message that must reach work on another host, or on hold, goes into its `messages.md`. | kept: [standards-messages.md#surfaces](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#surfaces) |
| R0309 | Recovering a session showing a red x | body | sentence | L578 | Do not open a new session first. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#recovering-a-session-showing-a-red-x](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#recovering-a-session-showing-a-red-x) |
| R0310 | Recovering a session showing a red x | body | sentence | L578 | Ask the same session to pick up the work identified by its closing thread line. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#recovering-a-session-showing-a-red-x](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#recovering-a-session-showing-a-red-x) |

| R0312 | Reply forms | body | sentence | L584 | Every reply to the owner takes one of three forms, which Kris set apart on 2026-10-16, as relayed: | kept: [standards-reporting.md#reply-forms](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#reply-forms); history to sources: REPORTING-FORMS |
| R0313 | Reply forms | body | sentence | L584 | Routine answers and acknowledgements should stay plain, without empty reporting sections or a "No reply needed" block. | to sources: REPORTING-FORMS |
| R0314 | Reply forms | body | sentence | L584 | When a completed-work report is required, use the owner-report format. | to sources: REPORTING-FORMS |

No heading, section, Needs tag or banner, except that a coordinator's reply that moves the mark, changes the work's state or answers where the work stands ends with the banner. In a conversational session, including regular or ad hoc work without a coordinating opener, it has no report heading, form line or banner, and opens at `## Done` | kept: [standards-reporting.md#reply-forms](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#reply-forms) |
| R0319 | Reply forms | body | row | L590 | Coordinator summary \| Only while explicitly coordinating a valid work package \| The report forms: the Summary, the re-bootstrap report and the hold report, each under the report heading and form line, closing with the banner and its thread line, with the work package and mark that go with them | kept: [standards-reporting.md#reply-forms](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#reply-forms) |
| R0320 | Reply forms | body | sentence | L594 | **Suggestions are recommendations with a confidence.** In every reply form, and in every session, coordinating or not, a suggestion is put as a recommendation carrying a confidence value, `high`, `medium` or `low`, as Kris set on 30 October 2026 under RECOMMEND-CONFIDENCE: | kept: [standards-reporting.md#reply-forms](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#reply-forms); history to sources: RECOMMEND-CONFIDENCE |
| R0321 | Session kinds | body | sentence | L598 | A session with no coordinating opener is conversational. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#session-kinds](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#session-kinds) |
| R0322 | Session kinds | body | sentence | L598 | It may do bounded work directly, including regular or ad hoc work, and uses the conversational reply forms. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#session-kinds](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#session-kinds) |
| R0323 | Session kinds | body | sentence | L598 | Kris separated this from coordinating work on 16 October 2026 under Reply forms; the repository declaring this skill or injecting its hook does not make the session a coordinator. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#session-kinds](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#session-kinds); history to sources: REPORTING-FORMS |
| R0324 | Session kinds | body | sentence | L600 | Coordination requires `ki-coordination` capability in the package-home repository, with its declared dependencies, unless a specific owner-approved exception expressly covers that opening. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#resolving-the-opener](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#resolving-the-opener) |

| R0327 | Units of work | body | sentence | L610 | **Run finished:** a background run the session launched has finished; for a background agent, its own run is ending. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#units-of-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#units-of-work) |
| R0328 | Units of work | body | sentence | L612 | **Stopped for the owner:** the work stopped before completion with something the owner must decide, supply or do, such as a failure, a block or a decision. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#units-of-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#units-of-work) |
| R0329 | Units of work | body | sentence | L614 | None of these ends a unit of work, and each gets a conversational reply: answering a question, however many reads it took; acknowledging an instruction or a decision; a clarifying question; an interim step or a timed update; a single read, lookup or check; a plan proposed in conversation for agreement. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#units-of-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#units-of-work) |
| R0330 | Units of work | body | sentence | L616 | A change the owner asked for and that was made in the same turn, in one file or one commit, with nothing failed, open or left to decide, is confirmed in one plain line naming what changed, and needs no report. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#units-of-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#units-of-work) |
| R0331 | Units of work | body | sentence | L616 | A background agent always reports, because its report is its run's only output. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#units-of-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#units-of-work) |
| R0332 | Units of work | body | sentence | L616 | One report covers every unit that ended in the same turn. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#units-of-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#units-of-work) |
| R0333 | Conversational bounded work | body | sentence | L620 | The work is bounded when it serves the matter the owner raised in the session, stays within the authority the owner gave there and each repository's own rules, and can finish with the owner in the session. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work) |
| R0334 | Conversational bounded work | body | sentence | L620 | The session may delegate when it chooses. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work) |
| R0335 | Conversational bounded work | body | sentence | L620 | It becomes a coordinator only when the owner opens or re-bootstraps it with a coordinating opener, never on its own. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work) |
| R0336 | Conversational bounded work | body | sentence | L622 | Work that must outlive the session takes an ad hoc work folder, `+/_WORK/<territory>.reg.<slug>/`, under the work-package standard, as Kris answered CONVERSATIONAL-RUN on 2026-10-16 by taking CONVRUN-NARROW's option a) for the run and folder it may take. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#conversational-bounded-work); history to sources: CONVERSATIONAL-RUN, CONVRUN-NARROW |

| R0406 | Owner report | body | sentence | L701 | The three labelled parts are separate paragraphs with a blank line between them, and each lettered option is its own paragraph with a blank line between options, as Kris chose FORM-VS-RUMDL a) on 26 October 2026, so that a query passes `rumdl check` under the house `.rumdl.toml`, whose MD013 reflow would otherwise join lazily continued lines into one; a report written before that day keeps its single-paragraph shape, and anything that reads past reports accepts both. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#owner-report](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#owner-report); history to sources: FORM-VS-RUMDL; tolerance kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#single-paragraph-queries-before-26-october](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#single-paragraph-queries-before-26-october) |
| R0407 | Owner report | body | sentence | L701 | Option a) is always the recommendation and carries `(recommended, confidence <level> - <reason>)` before its text, the level `high`, `medium` or `low` and the reason one line, such as `a) (recommended, confidence high - the patch is already on main) push it`. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#owner-report](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#owner-report) |

| R0411 | Owner report | body | sentence | L701 | The rubric's COORD-11 warns on any line of a report's Queries section that begins `<letter>) (recommended` without `, confidence high\|medium\|low - <reason>)`, in reports issued on or after 2026-10-18, as Kris agreed under CONFIDENCE-CHECK the same day: | kept: [standards-reporting.md#owner-report](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#owner-report); history to sources: CONFIDENCE-CHECK; tolerance kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#coord-11-confidence-cutover](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#coord-11-confidence-cutover) |

| R0415 | Owner report | body | sentence | L717 | **Posting outside:** a recommendation to post anything in Kris's name outside his own repositories and services, an issue, comment, reaction, pull request, discussion, review or message on another owner's repository or service such as an upstream issue tracker, is a query, never an action: its **Context** or its option carries the exact text proposed and where it would go, and nothing is posted until Kris answers that query; a broader grant, an earlier answer or a recommended option never stands in for it. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#owner-report](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#owner-report) |

So: | to sources: QUIET-TOOLS, RESTART-AGREE |

| R0641 | The Summary | body | sentence | L1142 | Sources are the work's decisions log, every report in the work folder and, for the reports a prune has deleted, their last revision in Git history, the parked tags in `discussions.md`, `ki agent status`, work-record transitions in the work's scope from the Git history of the roadmap paths, and `git log -- +/_WORK/<id>/`, the logbook. | kept: [standards-report-forms.md#the-summary](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-report-forms.md#the-summary) |
