| R0643 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1148 | **Work package:** the coordinator picks the work up from `+/_WORK/<territory>.prj.<project>/`, whose `work.md` defines the work and whose `state.md` holds its current state only, and keeps both current under the work-package standard. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0644 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1148 | The work file names its Project in front matter, and the Project note does not link down. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0645 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1149 | **Decisions:** it records each owner approval in `+/_WORK/<id>/decisions.md` before launching the work that approval authorises. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0646 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1150 | Releases follow the `ki-repo-tools` release-on-demand policy: a delivery run never releases by default, and a release run carries the `release` footer for the named release only. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0647 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1152 | **Opening:** open Project work only where there is active work to drive, within territory work's cap. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0648 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1152 | A dormant, registered Project may have an On hold package solely to receive its first message, under Seeding. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0649 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1153 | **Scope:** it works only its own Project's records. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0650 | Coordinating work | body | sentence | L1154 | **Close:** it shows Finished only at Finishing Project work; until then it moves between the other six states. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-work) |
| R0651 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1158 | Each territory Capital holds one main State of play, `+/_WORK/<territory>.sop.main/work.md`, titled `<Territory> > SoP`: the coordination of the territory's coordinations. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0652 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1158 | It may also hold secondary States of play, each coordinating a named part of the territory under the main. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0653 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1158 | Each does project management and may coordinate explicitly assigned Project packages: delivery remains in those packages and their product repositories, never in the State of play package itself. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0654 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1158 | No repository other than a Capital holds territory work. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0655 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1158 | The main is never Finished while the territory is open. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0656 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1158 | Where this standard says territory work without qualification, it means the main State of play; a secondary takes those duties only for the work it covers, as its section sets. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0657 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1163 | **Regular work:** it may discuss regular work and send it messages without opening coordination on that package; dispatch follows its explicit assignment and the owner-approved scope. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0658 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1164 | **Acting for idle work:** where a package in its territory has no active session, the State of play acts for it on its messages, as Kris set under SOP-ACTS-FOR-IDLE, raised at 15:50 GMT on 5 November 2026 and asked for at about 01:50 GMT on 6 November, relayed by `personal.reg.inbox-sol`: it acts for the package, "receiving, ACKing, routing and answering its messages, without necessarily taking its work forward". | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work); history to sources: SOP-ACTS-FOR-IDLE |
| R0659 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1164 | A package has an active session while its `listening_status` is `on`; otherwise nothing takes its messages until it is next opened, so the State of play takes them. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0660 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1164 | It takes the package's Inbound under Messages between work, IMPORTANT lines first and then in queue order, within one package per turn: it ACKs each line at the top of the sender's Inbound; routes a part that belongs to other work by a line in that work's `messages.md`, under Dispatch below; answers what the territory's coordination can answer by a reply line to the sender; and puts what needs the package's own judgement or the owner into the package's `state.md` next steps or `discussions.md`; then it removes the line in a commit naming the line and the package, `docs(work): <id> messages taken by <State of play id>`. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0661 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1164 | It takes the package's work forward only where its own assignment already covers it: acting for the package, it launches no run and changes none of its state, holder, `since`, mark, hold or decisions, and an On hold package stays on hold. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0662 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1164 | When the package's own session opens, it takes only the lines still in its queue and finds the dispositions in its files and Git, and the State of play stops acting for it. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0663 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1165 | Exceeding this existing active Project cap requires the owner's explicit agreement beforehand; the coordinator cannot raise it unilaterally. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0664 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1165 | Until then the default is to queue the next piece. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0665 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1165 | It keeps the active work and the ordered queue in its state file's `## Queue`, each with its title and opener, and opens the next queued piece when an active one goes on hold or finishes. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0666 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1166 | **Acceptance first:** at bootstrap and at resume, its first call on the owner is the territory's records awaiting acceptance, brought as one bundle per work under Acceptance bundles, before any other query. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0667 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1167 | **Seeding:** before Project work opens, territory work creates or reconciles its work folder and gives the owner its opener, so the session opens with one message. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0668 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1167 | The sender or the territory's State of play may create an absent, registered Project's package solely to queue its first message, within the receiving repository's authority. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0669 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1167 | Create all five package files with a minimal definition, empty queries and decisions, and `state: on-hold`, `hold.reason: paused` and `hold.release: when Kris opens this Project work`; a queued message does not open or automatically release the work. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0670 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1168 | **Flags:** at its own mark it names, by a line in each owning work's `messages.md`, every work folder the `ki-work-package` rubric's FOLDER items flag under Pruning the work folder, except a finished work's folder, which it never names in a message line; it never prunes another work folder itself, except that it closes a finished work's folder directly under the `ki-work-package` close-out and, if a close-out check fails, puts the query in its own `discussions.md`. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0671 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1168 | At the same mark it runs the worktree sweep and reports only what it finds. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0672 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1169 | **Other territories:** it coordinates with another territory's territory work only through the owner. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0673 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1169 | It never acts with authority in another territory's repositories; each Capital keeps its own ownership, and cross-repository work moves under `ki-trades` or the receiving repository's own process. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0674 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1171 | So actually improvements should really go back to state of play." Every new unit of work with no clear owner - an owner dump, a handoff with no owning work, a cross-work item whose owner is unclear, and every method improvement - comes to it, by the owner pasting a prompt there or by a line in its own `messages.md`. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work); history to sources: WORK-ROUTING |
| R0675 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1171 | A work's own follow-ups, an item in a record that already names its work and a reply to a message go direct and do not pass through it. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0676 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1173 | **Matching:** it matches the unit against the register, the `work.md` and `state.md` front matter `ki work status` derives from the work folders, reading a `work.md` body only where two descriptions match, and keeps no copy of the register. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0677 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1174 | **Routing:** it writes one dated line into the chosen work's `messages.md`, `- <date> · ROUTE · <Territory> > SoP (<territory>): <the unit in the owner's words, with links>`, at the position its priority warrants, and flags it important where it must be taken first, under Messages between work. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0678 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1175 | **Returns:** a part a work returns is routed once more at most; if it cannot be placed elsewhere, or would come back again, it goes to the owner as a query, so nothing bounces between works. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0679 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1178 | **Stewardship of continual improvement.** Every work tries to improve continually, and territory work coordinates it, as Kris amended STEWARD-HOME on 19 October: | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work); history to sources: STEWARD-HOME |
| R0680 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1178 | "everything should be trying to continually improve and I think that should be coordinated by state of play". | to sources: STEWARD-HOME |
| R0681 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1178 | Every work sends a method improvement to territory work by a `METHOD` line in its `messages.md`, and territory work watches the works for improvements itself. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0682 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1178 | While Ways of working is open, territory work routes each to it like any other unit, its description saying it takes method changes, and Ways of working sends targeted `REFRESH` lines under Refreshing this guidance. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0683 | Territory work | body | sentence | L1178 | When Ways of working finishes, territory work's `work.md` description gains method stewardship and its Scope says where method changes go: it keeps a method backlog, dispatches each accepted small improvement as small regular work or into the Project it most affects, with a design review for a change of shape, and sends the `REFRESH` lines itself; a significant change revives Ways of working, or a more targeted Project under the Model theme. | kept: [standards-territory.md#territory-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#territory-work) |
| R0684 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1182 | Surface absent, stale, inaccessible or ambiguous homes and unacknowledged ownership; do not take over or clear their messages. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0685 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1182 | The Capital's prose home reference is discovery, never a second package, state or inbox. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0686 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1182 | Reader compatibility is a prerequisite to automated member-home discovery; the local advisory hook does not scan the estate or guarantee runtime injection. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0687 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1184 | A State of play may steward an assigned package of any level only by the explicit committed assignment and acknowledged handover in the `ki-work-package` stewardship rule. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0688 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1184 | Its field remains the marked STEWARD-FIELD placeholder; do not add a field or reinterpret `worker`. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0689 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1184 | The old steward stops launches and reconciles runs in flight, queued launches and pending writes; the new steward acknowledges and takes their supervision before acting. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0690 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1184 | No absent session, missing binding, quiet thread or timeout transfers stewardship. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0691 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1184 | Assignment never changes the holder or releases On hold work. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0692 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1186 | An assigned State of play may use the package's own entry points, including tidying. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0693 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1186 | It may create or adopt a package only for a registered Project within the cap, with a state justified by the actual move. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0694 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1186 | A package assigned elsewhere receives only messages. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0695 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1186 | A territory hold drains assigned Project packages first and asks any independent steward to drain its own; it does not write another steward's package. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0696 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1188 | One territory Summary reports across the packages it stewards, naming the package on every item. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0697 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1188 | Each package keeps its own state, `since`, mark and beat. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0698 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1188 | A territory mark never moves Project marks, and acknowledgement moves only the work it clearly names. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0699 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1188 | Keep a decision spanning Projects once at the territory home and link it, rather than copying it as several grants. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0700 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1190 | A record's `project` resolves through the registry to its package and grants no authority. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0701 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1190 | Project-less work goes first to its existing regular or ad hoc owner, then to the State of play's routing queue only if unresolved. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0702 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1192 | Every run brief distinguishes its reporting package from the product repository; the launcher homes its report in that package whoever launched it, and for an Orca worker its coordinator copies the report there at receive, the one exception the `ki-delegation` run packet names. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0703 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1192 | Host requirements restrict device-dependent execution only: any coordinator may acknowledge, plan, ask about or dispatch a targeted line in IMPORTANT and queue order. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0704 | Coordinating assigned packages | body | sentence | L1192 | Keep unfulfilled requirements queued and counted, flagging their age when no eligible host has taken them; never infer a takeover. | kept: [standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#coordinating-assigned-packages) |
| R0705 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1196 | A territory always has its main State of play and may add secondary ones where it has more work than one coordinator can hold, as Kris set on 24 October 2026: a territory has a main State of play plus optional secondaries, such as `HNR > SoP (main)`, `HNR > SoP (5ge)` and `HNR > SoP (sites)`. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play); history to sources: SECONDARY-SOP |
| R0706 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1196 | A territory with no secondary needs no change. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0707 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1198 | Every package below the main names its coordinating State of play as `parent`. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0708 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1198 | Check parents against the register, same-territory membership, unique secondary coverage and cycles under the `ki-work-package` definition fields. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0709 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1198 | Work with no secondary stays with the main. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0710 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1199 | **Creating:** the owner decides each secondary and its coverage at the main, which records the decision and prepares the package On hold until the owner opens it; changing a coverage is a main decision, made in both `work.md` files in one commit. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0711 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1200 | **Front door:** the main stays the owner's only front door and the single dispatch coordinator. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0712 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1200 | Owner dumps, units with no clear owner, method improvements and anything touching more than one secondary, or a secondary and uncovered work, come to the main. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0713 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1201 | **Main owns:** the registry, membership and Known Lands, releases and priorities across secondaries, the territory-wide ceiling on active Project work, method stewardship, the worktree sweep and `REVIEW-HOLD`, and every decision touching more than one secondary. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0714 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1203 | A unit for one named covered work may go straight there. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0715 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1204 | A secondary sends cross-cutting matters to the main and may message another secondary directly where the owner of the matter is clear. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0716 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1204 | A secondary returns a part it cannot place to the main, once; the main then places it or asks the owner, so nothing bounces. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |
| R0717 | Secondary States of play | body | sentence | L1205 | **Acceptance and review:** each secondary brings the acceptance bundles for its covered work first at its own bootstrap and resume; the main brings the rest and shows each secondary's count. | kept: [standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-territory.md#secondary-states-of-play) |

| R0719 | Opening work | body | sentence | L1210 | In the runtime-specific Claude Code binding, the opener launches the session as `claude --name "<thread line>"`, `<role glyph> 🔑 <work-id>` from `work.md`'s `role`, or `🧵 <work-id>` with no role yet, the same name as the rename line and every report's thread line, as Kris decided RENAME-NAME a) on 1 November 2026 and STAGE-C-ROLES on 7 November 2026, and every bootstrap and re-bootstrap report carries one `Rename thread: /rename <thread line>` line, so a session opened another way can take the name; | kept: [standards-opening.md#opening-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-opening.md#opening-work); history to sources: RENAME-NAME, STAGE-C-ROLES |

| R0724 | Bootstrap | body | sentence | L1265 | **Before the opener.** On a fresh or changed machine the owner runs the `ki-bootstrap` machine-readiness checklist to its "Ready to coordinate" result: tools and level checkouts, the chezmoi binding of the coordinator hook and the `ki-coordinator` output style, `ki bootstrap` and `ki skill add ki-work-design`, the Capital's declarations, the observable checks that the hook and the style work, and `ki agent prompts <territory>` for the opener with its `claude --name` command. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap) |
| R0725 | Bootstrap | body | sentence | L1267 | A coordinator also re-bootstraps on request, after a compaction under Re-anchoring, after a model switch, and when picking work up under Picking work up. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap) |
| R0726 | Bootstrap | body | sentence | L1271 | resolves the exact id and actual repository to one authoritative work package, `+/_WORK/<id>/`, reads its five files, `messages.md` first, or its tolerated legacy record, and stops on an absent or ambiguous home; reconciles it under the work-package standard and checks `method_read`; checks the opener against its actual level (`territory`, `project` or `regular`) and scope, opening authority and package-home coordination capability under Session kinds, preserving a specifically approved exception such as Acquire intake; a mismatch or missing authority/capability without that exception gets a one-line explanation and the conversational reply forms, never automatic coordination; | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap); tolerance to [standards-coordination-migration.md#legacy-records-at-bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#legacy-records-at-bootstrap) |
| R0727 | Bootstrap | body | sentence | L1272 | acts on each line of its message queue, `messages.md`, important lines first, `FIRST-STEP` among them, then in queue order, before launching any work; | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap) |
| R0728 | Bootstrap | body | sentence | L1273 | as territory work, gathers every record awaiting acceptance across its territory's repositories, or as a secondary across the work it covers, and brings them first, ahead of every other query, as one bundle per work under Acceptance bundles; | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap) |

| R0730 | Bootstrap | body | sentence | L1275 | `## Done` gives its coordinator role and where the work stands and ends with the line `Rename thread: /rename <thread line>`, `<role glyph> 🔑 <work-id>` or `🧵 <work-id>` with no role yet, under Opening work, `## In Progress` what is running, and `## Queries` every open query in full; then it closes with the glyph banner and its thread line, rendered exactly as a Summary renders them, the thread line last. | kept: [standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-bootstrap.md#bootstrap) |

Kris set its route under ROUTE-UP and HOW-WE-WORK on 3 November 2026: urgent work goes straight to the live leader on the escalation path, "Jimmy is not in, so Bobby", without waiting on an ACK, and "not everything" is an escalation. | kept: [standards-messages.md#messages-between-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#messages-between-work); history to sources: URGENT-CRITERIA, ROUTE-UP, HOW-WE-WORK |
| R0754 | Messages between work | body | sentence | L1289 | So the sender, first, hands it to the owning work's live worker where one is live, as an `URGENT` line in that work's `messages.md`; otherwise, second, it writes the same line for the record and at once sends it to the nearest live leader up the owner's `parent` chain, its State of play and then the territory's main State of play, which judges the urgency and delegates within its own authority; third, whoever takes it writes the ACK line back to the sender; and fourth, only where no leader on the path is live and the unit lies within the sender's own authority, the sender does it through a detached run and then replaces its original line with a `SUPERSEDED` line naming the run and its commits, so the owner can adjust or tidy when it next comes online. | kept: [standards-messages.md#messages-between-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-messages.md#messages-between-work); history to sources: ROUTE-UP, HOW-WE-WORK |

| R0787 | Decisions life cycle | body | sentence | L1328 | A struck entry leaves no stub, and entries carry no running numbers. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#decisions-life-cycle](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#decisions-life-cycle) |

| R0789 | Decisions life cycle | body | sentence | L1330 | A design loop's own decisions file is a stage artefact that holds only the answers to its report's tagged decisions, and every one of those answers is also a decisions-log entry, logged in the turn it arrived. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#decisions-life-cycle](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#decisions-life-cycle) |
| R0790 | Design notes | body | sentence | L1334 | Design notes of every kind - options papers, mocks, loop reports and their stage decisions - live in the work folder of the work whose concern they design, in the folder of the focus area they serve, `+/_WORK/<id>/<focus-id>/`, or in `+/_WORK/<id>/design/` in a package that declares no focus areas, under the `ki-work-design` standard and the `ki-work-package` closed list. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#design-notes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#design-notes) |
| R0791 | Design notes | body | sentence | L1334 | No `design/` folder sits anywhere under `Streams/`, which holds only definitions and records. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#design-notes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#design-notes) |
| R0792 | Design notes | body | sentence | L1334 | Design work with no coordinating work of its own takes an ad hoc work folder. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#design-notes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#design-notes); stage 5: review S5-D01 applied, "no coordinating work of its own" kept |
| R0793 | Design notes | body | sentence | L1334 | The earlier `+/_DESIGN/<subject-slug>/` home, set with "DESIGN-RETENTION - (a) +/_DESIGN/<subject-slug>/ in every repository", is read for the tolerance window and moves with its territory's migration. | kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#the-earlier-design-note-home](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#the-earlier-design-note-home) |
| R0794 | Design notes | body | sentence | L1336 | A design note's life cycle - draft, under review, graduated or deleted - and the rule that nothing cites a deleted note are the `ki-work-design` artefact home's, which owns graduation; the `ki-work-package` closed list sets which file kinds a focus folder or `design/` may hold. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#design-notes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#design-notes) |
| R0795 | Design notes | body | sentence | L1336 | This standard reviews the design notes at each mark, under Pruning the work folder, and resolves it before Project work finishes. | kept: [standards-decisions.md#design-notes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#design-notes) |
| R0796 | Finishing Project work | body | sentence | L1340 | Project work moves to Finished once only, on the owner's `finish`, after the close-out Kris confirmed with "confirm the four close-out steps": | kept: [standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work); history to sources: FINISH-CLOSE-OUT |
| R0797 | Finishing Project work | body | sentence | L1342 | every logged decision is homed under the decisions life cycle, with none left homeless or held; | kept: [standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work) |
| R0798 | Finishing Project work | body | sentence | L1343 | its design notes are resolved, in its focus folders or `design/`: every note graduated and deleted; | kept: [standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work) |
| R0799 | Finishing Project work | body | sentence | L1345 | Where the folder outlives the finishing thread, territory work closes it at an owner-authorised mark of its own whose report names the folder, under that close-out, rather than naming it in the finished work's `messages.md`, which no coordinator reads, as Kris asked under FINISHED-TIDY-KICK on 30 October 2026: | kept: [standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work); history to sources: FINISHED-TIDY-KICK |
| R0800 | Finishing Project work | body | sentence | L1347 | Before the Finished banner, every Orca worker the work started is settled under Closing out Orca workers. | kept: [standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#finishing-project-work) |
| R0801 | Working rules | body | sentence | L1353 | These rules apply to every coordinator: territory work and each piece of Project work. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#working-rules](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#working-rules) |
| R0802 | The mark | body | sentence | L1357 | The mark is only a timestamp: the default start of the next Summary, as Kris approved under SUMMARY-MODEL on 4 November 2026. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark); history to sources: SUMMARY-MODEL |
| R0803 | The mark | body | sentence | L1357 | A Summary is distinct from `ki-recap`, which summarises one session at a lower level; the two share no format or procedure. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0804 | The mark | body | sentence | L1359 | At each mark, perform the repository level check before reporting the mark. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0805 | The mark | body | sentence | L1361 | Each piece of work has one mark, and striking a new one replaces it; | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0806 | The mark | body | sentence | L1361 | The banner and the mark describe or date a decision and never cite a number, because a number, in Kris's words of 2026-10-14, "has no meaning to me because I don't know what the decision was ... and I don't know where the list is". | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark); history to sources: DECISION-NO-NUMBER; stage 5: merged into [standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#coordinating-practices) and [standards-decisions.md#decisions-log](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-decisions.md#decisions-log) |
| R0807 | The mark | body | sentence | L1362 | **Striking:** only the owner moves the mark, by saying `mark`, `mark only` or `mark, status only`, and a hold strikes one. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0808 | The mark | body | sentence | L1362 | Giving a Summary or a Status does not move it. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0809 | The mark | body | sentence | L1363 | **Summary after the mark:** `mark`, and every mark struck at the end of a query set, is followed at once, in the same reply, by the Summary since the previous mark, with every open query in full, so Kris never has to ask for it; | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0810 | The mark | body | sentence | L1363 | `mark only` strikes it with no Summary, and `mark, status only` strikes it and gives the Status, the Summary without its history. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0811 | The mark | body | sentence | L1363 | Kris set the Summary after the mark on 31 October 2026 under MARK-THEN-SUMMARY: | to sources: MARK-THEN-SUMMARY |
| R0812 | The mark | body | sentence | L1363 | "you take mark, can you tell me basically you're expecting always summary in full after mark has been taken? | to sources: MARK-THEN-SUMMARY |
| R0813 | The mark | body | sentence | L1363 | That's I expect", and added `mark only` under SUMMARY-MODEL. | to sources: MARK-THEN-SUMMARY, SUMMARY-MODEL |
| R0814 | The mark | body | sentence | L1364 | Outstanding carries forward across marks: striking a mark resets only the window of `## Summary since <time>`, and never drops, closes or hides an open item, which the Summary's status table and `## Queries` carry in full. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0815 | The mark | body | sentence | L1365 | **History:** the work's history comes from the logbook and the run reports, never from `state.md`, as Kris took STATE-HISTORY on 19 October. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark); history to sources: STATE-HISTORY |
| R0816 | The mark | body | sentence | L1365 | `state.md` holds no dated log, commit runs or test counts, and may link one report or commit rather than copy it, which the `ki-work-package` rubric's history check warns on. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0817 | The mark | body | sentence | L1366 | **No mark yet:** a Summary runs from the thread's start, and the first `mark` strikes the first mark; where the thread's start is unknown, it runs from `work.md`'s `created_at`. | kept: [standards-mark.md#the-mark](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#the-mark) |
| R0818 | Parking tangents | body | sentence | L1370 | It does not explore the tangent unasked. | kept: [standards-mark.md#parking-tangents](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#parking-tangents) |
| R0819 | Parking tangents | body | sentence | L1372 | Parked tangents live in `discussions.md`'s `## Parked` section beside the parked queries, each as `- Parked <YYYY-MM-DD>: <one line>`, under the work-package standard; work waiting on a named decision or event goes to `state.md`'s `## Held` with its release condition, and deferred deliverable work becomes a triage record in its home repository under `ki-next`'s capture rule, as Kris took HELD-VS-RECORD a) on 7 November 2026. | kept: [standards-mark.md#parking-tangents](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#parking-tangents); history to sources: HELD-VS-RECORD |
| R0820 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | It links each one to the local file by absolute path, so that it opens in the owner's editor, resolving the repository through the KI registry (`ki registry`) where one is available. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records) |
| R0821 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | It uses the verified canonical remote URL only when there is no local copy. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records) |
| R0822 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | A link is never invented: when a target cannot be resolved, the text says so. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records) |
| R0823 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | A pruned roadmap record has no local file any more, so it is linked as `[<FULL-ID> (pruned)](https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>/blob/<last revision>/<path>)`: the link text is always the full identifier followed by `(pruned)`, and the last revision is the last commit in which the file existed, usually the parent of the prune commit. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records) |
| R0824 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | A live record keeps its local-file link; a pruned record is never pointed at the Project note that carries it and never left as a bare identifier. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records) |
| R0825 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | The rubric's COORD-7 checks the reports under `+/_WORK/<id>/`, in each focus folder its `work.md` declares and in `reports/`, and under `+/_THREADS/<run>/` for the tolerance window: every full record identifier in their prose, a work record or a Decision Record, sits inside a Markdown link, and a link that names a record and targets an absolute local path points at a file that exists. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records); tolerance kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#coord-7-and-thread-folders](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#coord-7-and-thread-folders) |
| R0826 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | The existence check applies inside the audited checkout and wherever the target's folder is present on the machine, so another checkout beside it is checked too; a target outside the checkout whose folder is absent, as every other repository is on a CI runner, is checked for shape only, a Markdown file whose name opens with the linked identifier, as KI-HARNESS-GOV-197 (pruned) set. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records); stage 5: the KI-HARNESS-GOV-197 citation kept |
| R0827 | Linking records | body | sentence | L1380 | Fenced blocks, inline code spans, which quote literal text such as a commit subject and cannot hold a link, and Needs tags such as `ACCEPT-181` are not record identifiers and are not checked. | kept: [standards-reporting.md#linking-records](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-reporting.md#linking-records) |
| R0828 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1384 | The work folder `+/_WORK/<id>/` is tracked, and the knowledge in it is safe only once it is in Git on a remote. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder) |
| R0829 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1384 | "threads need to know that they need to commit their work under +/_THREAD/", and the work model made every send a commit: | to sources: COMMIT-WORK-FOLDER |
| R0830 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1384 | "The work outlives every session and is committed at every send." So a coordinator commits and pushes its work folder at every send, every owner receive, every mark and hold and before the work finishes, by explicit path and never with another session's uncommitted changes, each commit's subject carrying the move; a line it writes into other work's `messages.md` is committed and pushed at once. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder); history to sources: COMMIT-WORK-FOLDER |
| R0831 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1384 | Every such commit, and every message write, first passes the repository's own checks under the `ki-git` checks before committing rule. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder) |
| R0832 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1386 | **Cadence.** Kris tightened this on 25 October 2026, choosing COMMIT-CADENCE a), "Adopt cadence", after work sat uncommitted in the folder: | to sources: COMMIT-CADENCE |
| R0833 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1386 | So every session, coordinator or run, commits the work package by explicit path after every finished change, before its turn ends, and a run commits each step as it goes. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder); history to sources: COMMIT-CADENCE |
| R0834 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1386 | It then runs a **git sync**, `git fetch`, a fast-forward pull into a clean checkout and a push under Pushing beneath other sessions, after every finished change and at least every 15 minutes while it works, as Kris asked the same night: | kept: [standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder); history to sources: COMMIT-CADENCE |
| R0835 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1386 | "every change that's finished, really, we should be looking to do the sink, or every 15 minutes or something like that". | to sources: COMMIT-CADENCE |
| R0836 | Committing the work folder | body | sentence | L1386 | A pull that cannot fast-forward goes to the conflict run that section sets. | kept: [standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination.md#committing-the-work-folder) |
| R0837 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1390 | At every send and at each mark the coordinator prunes its own work folder, so that `+/_WORK/<id>/` shows only what is still open and Git history holds the rest, as the note's Shared history is never rewritten sets. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0838 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1390 | What goes, what stays, how and who are the `ki-work-package` Tidying the work folder rules, over the folder's closed list: the five package files always stay; this standard keeps their timing and the judgement each needs. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0839 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1392 | **Judgement:** whether the report's outcome, or every point of a feedback package, has a durable home in a work record, decisions log, work package or commit. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0840 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1392 | In a five-file package, a report is eligible for its owning steward's tidy once its outcome is homed and every query has been copied in full into `discussions.md`; those queries need not already be answered. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0841 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1392 | In the single-file tolerance shape, retain the report until its queries are answered and read back in the log, under `ki-work-package` Tidying the work folder. | kept: [standards-coordination-migration.md#single-file-work-package-shapes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-coordination-migration.md#single-file-work-package-shapes) |
| R0842 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1392 | Keep every report whose outcome has no durable home yet, without homing it in haste; a later Summary reads a deleted report from its last revision. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0843 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1393 | **Design notes:** at each mark the coordinator reviews the design notes in its focus folders, or `design/`, and deletes each note that has graduated under the `ki-work-design` artefact home, or that the work package records as superseded; drafts, notes under review and any in doubt stay. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0844 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1393 | The mark prompts the review, never an automatic deletion. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0845 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1393 | Beyond graduation, a note goes only when every outcome it carried is homed, the owner's words and any grant included, and every link into it has been rewritten first; a diagram's source and its renders go together. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0846 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1394 | **Temporary output:** the coordinator deletes, by explicit path, each `tmp/<run>/` folder of its own whose output is superfluous. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0847 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1395 | **Record threshold:** the deletion is operational work under the record threshold and opens no record. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0848 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1396 | **Flagging:** the `ki-work-package` rubric's FOLDER items fail a work folder holding a file outside the closed list and list as candidates for the prune every report whose Needs tags the decisions log all names, every brief whose run's report is received, every finished work's folder and an unmatched `+/_WORK_REVIEW/` folder or index line; whether each candidate's outcome has a durable home stays the owning coordinator's judgement, and the listing is never permission to delete. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0849 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1396 | At its own mark territory work names each flagged folder by a line in that work's `messages.md`, and the owning coordinator prunes it at its next send, except a finished work's folder, which territory work closes itself under the `ki-work-package` close-out instead of naming it in a message line. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0850 | Pruning the work folder | body | sentence | L1396 | FOLDER-7 warns on a decisions log the `ki agent close` gate cannot read as `##` entries alone. | kept: [standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#pruning-the-work-folder) |
| R0851 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1400 | A coordinator that starts Orca supervised workers, or whose runs start them, closes out each settled worker as part of its close-out, because Orca's own orchestration guide counts supervised work as done only when "each settled worker was reused, explicitly retained, or released". | kept: [standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers) |
| R0852 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1400 | Kris asked for this through the ORCA-WORKER-CLEANUP message of 30 October 2026, and ORCA-CLOSE-OUT a) was taken under ACCEPT-BY-CRITERIA on 31 October 2026, on its blind review's condition that Orca's steps and the effect of release be checked before the wording became normative. | to sources: ORCA-WORKER-CLEANUP, ORCA-CLOSE-OUT, ACCEPT-BY-CRITERIA |
| R0853 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1400 | They were checked, read only, against Orca 1.4.244's `worker-release`, `worker-retain` and `worker-list` help and its orchestration guide with the `recovery-and-cleanup` reference. | to sources: ORCA-CLOSE-OUT |
| R0854 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1402 | **Receive:** when the coordinator receives the report of an Orca worker it or its runs started, and Orca shows the Dispatch settled, succeeded or failed, it does exactly one of three things and names which in the receive commit: reuses the same terminal for an immediate follow-up Dispatch, retains it with `orca orchestration worker-retain --dispatch <id> --json` only at the owner's explicit request, or releases it with `orca orchestration worker-release --dispatch <id> --json`. | kept: [standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers) |
| R0855 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1402 | Release is the default, and it happens as soon as the worker's completion message is received and acknowledged, in the same wake, never deferred to the mark, so that a finished worker is not left open for the owner to raise. | kept: [standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers) |
| R0856 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1402 | `hnr.reg.email-routing` already releases each worker from its waiter on the completion message. | to sources: WORKER-LEFTOVERS |
| R0857 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1403 | **Authority:** only an accepted settlement authorises release. | kept: [standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers) |
| R0858 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1403 | `terminal close` is never a substitute, and the recovery action of a `release_pending` or `release_unknown` receipt is followed exactly and reported. | kept: [standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers) |
| R0859 | Closing out Orca workers | body | sentence | L1404 | **Mark, hold and Finished:** for each Orca Run the work created, `orca orchestration worker-list --run <run id> --terminal-state reclaimable --json` returns none before a mark is struck, before a hold report and before the Finished banner; a settled worker the receive step left open, a leftover, is released then under the same authority and reported. | kept: [standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-mark.md#closing-out-orca-workers) |

| R0922 | Unattended windows | body | sentence | L1450 | Unanswered, it is parked as a query for the return report, keeping its tag, Context, Query and Options, nothing is done on the matter it raised in either direction, and the other authorised work carries on; silence is never consent, and no default or recommended option stands in for the answer. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows) |
| R0923 | Unattended windows | body | sentence | L1450 | "If you ask me for input as well in ... an unattended period, you should only really ask it for a couple of minutes. | to sources: WINDOW-QUESTIONS |
| R0924 | Unattended windows | body | sentence | L1450 | And if I don't respond, then you should not take any action from it", under the `ki-delegation` questions rule. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows); history to sources: WINDOW-QUESTIONS |
| R0925 | Unattended windows | body | sentence | L1452 | **Return:** the return report is the Summary since the window's mark, extended across the repositories the window touched: this work's own units in detail, other sessions' commits counted against the revisions recorded at entry, each failure beside its unit, and a closing list of misses, such as usage-limit stops, missed processing and failed runs. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows) |
| R0926 | Unattended windows | body | sentence | L1452 | An optional interactive walk-through follows, under **Questions** in Coordinating practices, then strike the next mark only once the owner has seen the return report or completed the walk-through. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#unattended-windows) |
| R0927 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1456 | A headless pass is one bounded coordinator turn run without a live session: a machine scheduler starts it, it reads one work package, does only what that package already authorises, commits and exits. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0928 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1456 | It is the durable wake a session-only schedule cannot give, since a session's schedule fires only while the session is idle and dies with it. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0929 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1456 | The job's design and install belong to Rig, chezmoi and tools-ki, under the `ki-delegation` dotfiles and software rule; this standard sets what a pass may do and installs nothing. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0930 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1458 | **Trigger:** the scheduled job is shell-only and spends no model time of its own. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0931 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1458 | For each work it watches it asks three questions: has the work's `messages.md` changed since the job's last stamp, has one of the work's runs finished under `ki agent status`, has a deadline the package records passed, such as a window's end, a hold's release date or a dated next step? | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0932 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1458 | If none has, it writes its stamp and exits. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0933 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1458 | A firing on a Mac that was asleep is coalesced into one, under launchd's `StartCalendarInterval`, so a pass never fires more than once for a missed interval. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0934 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1459 | **Which work:** a pass runs only for work that names it: an open unattended window whose grant names headless passes, or a standing decision in the work's own decisions log that names them and what they may do. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0935 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1459 | Work that names no pass is stamped and left alone, and the change it carries waits for the next attended wake. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0936 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1459 | A pass never runs for work On hold or Finished. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0937 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1460 | **What a pass may do:** exactly what the coordinator does at a wake, within the turn budget: read the package, the five files and every open finding; act on each `messages.md` line in queue order under Messages between work; receive each finished run, copying its queries into `discussions.md`, recording its disposition and tidying under Pruning the work folder; launch only the units the plan in `state.md`'s next steps and the recorded decisions already authorise, through `ki agent`; record each move in the decisions log and `state.md`; commit the package by explicit path and sync under Committing the work folder. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0938 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1460 | It asks no question and waits on no answer: whatever needs Kris becomes a query in `discussions.md`, and nothing is done on that matter in either direction, under the window rule that silence is never consent. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0939 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1460 | It never opens, releases or finishes work, accepts or prunes, adopts or promotes, answers its own queries, widens a grant, launches a unit the plan does not name, edits another work's folder beyond one `messages.md` line, or runs a command that may wait on a permission prompt. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0940 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1461 | **Authority:** a pass carries no authority of its own; it acts within the grant or decision that names it, under the `ki-delegation` authority tiers, its unattended deny list and criticality test. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0941 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1461 | Its own tier is `push` at most: it commits the package and syncs under the standing push rule, fast-forward only, never forced and never with `--no-verify`, and hands a pull that cannot fast-forward to a conflict run. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0942 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1461 | A `release` or `prune` tier belongs to a run the pass launches with its cited decision, never to the pass itself. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0943 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1461 | A pass that finds its grant expired does only the receive bookkeeping, copying queries and recording finished runs, and launches nothing. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0944 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1462 | It spends model time only when the trigger found something new, keeps to the five-tool-call turn, and stops when the queue is acted on, at a wall-clock limit the job sets, or at ten turns, whichever comes first; what it has not reached waits for the next firing. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0945 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1462 | It never retries inside one firing. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0946 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1463 | A pass never runs for work whose coordinator session is live; how the job tells liveness is its design, and until that is settled a live coordinator stops the job for its work with "stop the wake". | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0947 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1463 | Passes for different works may run together only in different repositories; passes writing the same repository are serialised, under the `ki-git` write window. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0948 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1463 | Before writing, a pass re-reads any package file whose last commit is newer than its read, under the stale-edit rule, and keeps the other writer's change. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0949 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1464 | **Unavailable capacity:** when the model cannot be reached, because the plan's usage limit is hit, the provider is down or the runner cannot authenticate, the pass exits without acting, writes the reason to the job's log and leaves its stamp unchanged, so the same trigger fires the next pass. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0950 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1464 | It retries only at the next scheduled firing, never sooner, and never backs off further. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0951 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1464 | When `ki agent` has no worker capacity, the pass queues the unit under the run queue and does not wait. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0952 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1464 | Each stop is a miss the next attended report lists; a trigger is not processed work. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0953 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1465 | **Report:** a pass sends nothing to Kris and writes no owner report. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0954 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1465 | The next attended Summary or return report carries what passes did since the mark under the Summary, with each miss beside its unit: usage-limit stops, skipped firings, passes that found a live session and runs that failed. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |
| R0955 | Headless passes | body | sentence | L1466 | A pass stops a run only when Kris has named it and said stop, as any coordinator does. | kept: [standards-unattended.md#headless-passes](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-unattended.md#headless-passes) |

| R0988 | Pushing beneath other sessions | body | sentence | L1512 | "as a standing rule, a session may push everything stacked on `main`, other sessions' commits included, fast-forward only, never forced, never `--no-verify`, stopping on divergence; this supersedes the 14 and 19 October own-commits-only rule". | to sources: PACKAGE-PUSH |
| R0989 | Pushing beneath other sessions | body | sentence | L1514 | **Commit:** every session, a coordinator or a background agent, commits its own work by explicit path, under the `ki-delegation` coordination rules, and never commits, reverts or stashes another session's files. | kept: [standards-sync.md#pushing-beneath-other-sessions](../../../../skills/change-management/ki-coordination/references/standards-sync.md#pushing-beneath-other-sessions) |
